"""
Teste de integração OPT-IN do BackblazeB2UsageService.

Este teste NÃO roda por padrão. Ele só roda quando `B2_INTEGRATION=1`
está definido no ambiente. Sem isso, é pulado.

COMPORTAMENTO
-------------
    B2_INTEGRATION != '1'                        → skip
    B2_INTEGRATION == '1' + credenciais ausentes → FAIL (não skip)
    B2_INTEGRATION == '1' + credenciais válidas  → roda contra a API real

O fail (em vez de skip) é intencional: se você pediu explicitamente o
teste de integração, uma configuração ausente é um erro que precisa ser
visto — não escondido.

OPERAÇÕES
---------
    Somente leitura.
    - `fetch()` chama a API do B2 via boto3 (`list_objects_v2`).
    - `sync()` chama `fetch()` e persiste o snapshot no banco de testes
      (SQLite em memória durante o pytest).

Nenhuma operação de escrita é feita no B2. Não há upload, delete, nem
`put_object`. O único efeito colateral é local ao banco de teste.

COMO RODAR
----------
    # Só os testes de integração
    B2_INTEGRATION=1 pytest -m integration

    # Todos os demais (integração pulada)
    pytest -m "not integration"

    # Ou sem o marker
    pytest apps/console/tests/ -v

NOTA SOBRE O QUE É VALIDADO
---------------------------
Este teste NÃO assume valores específicos do B2. Não há garantia de
que um bucket esteja vazio ou cheio. O que é validado é apenas:
    - A chamada funciona e devolve `ok=True`.
    - O payload tem as chaves esperadas.
    - Os tipos das métricas estão corretos quando presentes.
    - `sync()` persiste corretamente o snapshot.

Asserções sobre valores numéricos específicos ficam fora — elas
seriam frágeis e dependeriam do estado real do bucket.
"""

import os
from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from django.conf import settings
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.console.models import StorageProviderSnapshot
from apps.console.services.b2_usage_service import (
    BackblazeB2UsageService,
    PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
    PAYLOAD_VERSION,
)


# =========================================================================
# GUARD DE OPT-IN
# =========================================================================

_INTEGRATION_ENABLED = os.getenv('B2_INTEGRATION') == '1'


pytestmark = [
    pytest.mark.integration,
    pytest.mark.skipif(
        not _INTEGRATION_ENABLED,
        reason=(
            'Teste de integração real do B2 desabilitado. '
            'Defina B2_INTEGRATION=1 para rodar.'
        ),
    ),
]


# =========================================================================
# VALIDAÇÃO DE CREDENCIAIS E BUCKET
# =========================================================================

def _assert_credentials_configured():
    """
    Verifica que as credenciais e o bucket estão configurados.

    Chamado em cada teste quando B2_INTEGRATION=1. Se algo estiver
    faltando, LEVANTA AssertionError — o teste falha, não pula.
    Isso é intencional: foi pedido o teste de integração, uma
    configuração ausente é um erro que precisa ser visto.
    """
    assert BackblazeB2UsageService.is_available(), (
        'B2_INTEGRATION=1 mas credenciais ausentes. Defina '
        'B2_KEY_ID, B2_APPLICATION_KEY, B2_BUCKET_NAME, '
        'B2_ENDPOINT e B2_REGION no .env.'
    )

    bucket = getattr(settings, 'B2_BUCKET_NAME', None)
    assert bucket, (
        'B2_INTEGRATION=1 mas settings.B2_BUCKET_NAME está vazio.'
    )
    assert isinstance(bucket, str), (
        f'settings.B2_BUCKET_NAME deveria ser string, '
        f'recebido {type(bucket).__name__}.'
    )


# =========================================================================
# IS_AVAILABLE — verificação de configuração real
# =========================================================================

class TestB2IntegrationAvailability:

    def test_is_available_returns_true(self):
        """
        Se B2_INTEGRATION=1, a configuração do ambiente deve estar
        completa. Se `is_available()` devolver False, é bug de
        configuração — o teste falha.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()
        assert BackblazeB2UsageService.is_available() is True

    def test_bucket_name_is_set(self):
        """O bucket name deve estar preenchido em settings."""
        _assert_credentials_configured()
        bucket = settings.B2_BUCKET_NAME
        assert isinstance(bucket, str)
        assert len(bucket) > 0


# =========================================================================
# FETCH — contra a API real
# =========================================================================

class TestB2IntegrationFetch:

    def test_fetch_returns_ok(self):
        """
        A chamada real à API do B2 deve retornar `ok=True`.

        Se o B2 estiver inacessível ou as credenciais estiverem
        erradas, o teste falha — porque a intenção de rodar a
        integração foi explícita.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is True, (
            f'Chamada real ao B2 falhou: {result["error"]}'
        )
        assert result['error'] is None
        assert isinstance(result['payload'], dict)

    def test_fetch_payload_has_expected_keys(self):
        """
        O payload deve ter as chaves que o serviço produz.
        Não asserção sobre valores — só sobre a estrutura.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()
        assert result['ok'] is True

        payload = result['payload']
        expected_keys = {
            'payload_version',
            'bucket_name',
            'endpoint',
            'region',
            'total_bytes',
            'file_count',
            'truncated',
        }
        assert expected_keys.issubset(payload.keys())

    def test_fetch_payload_version_is_current(self):
        """O payload_version deve ser o que o serviço define."""
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()
        assert result['ok'] is True
        assert result['payload']['payload_version'] == PAYLOAD_VERSION

    def test_fetch_payload_types_are_correct(self):
        """
        Valida os TIPOS quando os campos estão presentes.

        Não presume valores — só verifica coerência de tipo:
            - total_bytes: int >= 0
            - file_count: int >= 0
            - truncated: bool
            - bucket_name: string não vazia
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()
        assert result['ok'] is True

        payload = result['payload']

        total_bytes = payload['total_bytes']
        assert isinstance(total_bytes, int)
        assert total_bytes >= 0

        file_count = payload['file_count']
        assert isinstance(file_count, int)
        assert file_count >= 0

        truncated = payload['truncated']
        assert isinstance(truncated, bool)

        bucket_name = payload['bucket_name']
        assert isinstance(bucket_name, str)
        assert len(bucket_name) > 0

    def test_fetch_payload_bucket_matches_settings(self):
        """
        O bucket reportado pelo payload deve ser o mesmo bucket
        configurado em settings. Isso é o que a sua precaução
        pediu: validar que estamos olhando o bucket esperado.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()
        assert result['ok'] is True
        assert result['payload']['bucket_name'] == settings.B2_BUCKET_NAME


# =========================================================================
# SYNC — persiste o snapshot no banco de teste
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestB2IntegrationSync:

    def test_sync_persists_ok_snapshot(self):
        """
        `sync()` deve persistir um snapshot com `status='ok'` e
        `provider_key=backblaze_b2`.

        O snapshot é persistido no banco de teste (SQLite em memória),
        não no B2. Nenhuma escrita é feita no bucket.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot is not None
        assert snapshot.provider_key == PROVIDER_KEY_B2
        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK
        assert snapshot.fetched_at is not None

    def test_sync_persists_storage_usage_bytes(self):
        """
        O snapshot persistido deve ter `storage_usage_bytes`
        preenchido e compatível com o payload.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK
        # Deve ser um inteiro não-negativo — o valor exato depende do
        # estado do bucket, e não é presumido pelo teste.
        assert isinstance(snapshot.storage_usage_bytes, int)
        assert snapshot.storage_usage_bytes >= 0

    def test_sync_persists_raw_payload(self):
        """
        O `raw_payload` deve estar presente com as chaves esperadas.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK
        assert isinstance(snapshot.raw_payload, dict)

        payload = snapshot.raw_payload
        assert payload['bucket_name'] == settings.B2_BUCKET_NAME
        assert payload['payload_version'] == PAYLOAD_VERSION

    def test_sync_does_not_fill_cloudinary_fields(self):
        """
        Os campos específicos do Cloudinary devem permanecer NULL,
        porque o B2 não tem equivalentes.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK
        assert snapshot.plan_name == ''
        assert snapshot.credits_usage is None
        assert snapshot.credits_limit is None
        assert snapshot.credits_used_percent is None
        assert snapshot.bandwidth_usage_bytes is None
        assert snapshot.transformations_usage is None
        assert snapshot.objects_count is None
        assert snapshot.media_limits is None

    def test_sync_is_append_only_against_real_b2(self):
        """
        Cada chamada de `sync()` cria uma linha nova. Verifica
        contra a API real que o modelo não faz upsert.
        """
        _assert_credentials_configured()

        before = StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.filter(
            provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
        ).count()

        s1 = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()
        s2 = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        after = StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.filter(
            provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
        ).count()

        assert after == before + 2
        assert s1.id != s2.id

"""
Testes unitários do BackblazeB2UsageService.

Todos usam mock do boto3 — nenhuma chamada de rede real.

O serviço chama `boto3.client(...)` dentro de `fetch()`, então o
mock precisa patchear `boto3.client` no módulo `boto3` em si
(não no módulo do serviço).

A validação contra a API real do B2 fica em
`test_b2_usage_service_integration.py`, que só roda com `B2_INTEGRATION=1`.
"""

from datetime import timedelta
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest
from botocore.exceptions import (
    BotoCoreError,
    ClientError,
    EndpointConnectionError,
)
from django.test import override_settings
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.console.models import StorageProviderSnapshot
from apps.console.services.b2_usage_service import (
    BackblazeB2UsageService,
    PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
    PAYLOAD_VERSION,
)


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS
# =========================================================================

B2_SETTINGS = dict(
    B2_CONFIGURED=True,
    B2_KEY_ID='test-key',
    B2_APPLICATION_KEY='test-app-key',
    B2_BUCKET_NAME='test-bucket',
    B2_ENDPOINT='https://s3.us-east-005.backblazeb2.com',
    B2_REGION='us-east-005',
)


def _make_client_error(code: str, message: str) -> ClientError:
    """Constrói um ClientError com payload mínimo."""
    return ClientError(
        {'Error': {'Code': code, 'Message': message}},
        operation_name='ListObjectsV2',
    )


def _make_paginator(contents_per_page):
    """
    Cria um mock de paginador do boto3 que devolve as páginas na ordem.

    `contents_per_page`: lista de listas de dicts. Cada item da lista
    externa é uma página. Cada item da interna é um objeto S3.
    """
    paginator = MagicMock()
    paginator.paginate.return_value = [
        {'Contents': page} for page in contents_per_page
    ]
    return paginator


def _make_client_with_paginator(paginator):
    """Cria um mock de client S3 cujo `get_paginator` devolve o paginador."""
    client = MagicMock()
    client.get_paginator.return_value = paginator
    return client


# =========================================================================
# IS_AVAILABLE
# =========================================================================

class TestIsAvailable:

    @override_settings(B2_CONFIGURED=True)
    def test_true_when_flag_true(self):
        assert BackblazeB2UsageService.is_available() is True

    @override_settings(B2_CONFIGURED=False)
    def test_false_when_flag_false(self):
        assert BackblazeB2UsageService.is_available() is False

    def test_fallback_checks_env_when_flag_absent(self, settings):
        """
        Sem a flag, o serviço faz fallback para `os.getenv`.

        Aqui manipulamos `os.environ` diretamente porque o fallback
        lê de env, não de settings.
        """
        import os

        settings.B2_CONFIGURED = None
        # `hasattr` retorna True mesmo se for None, então o teste
        # precisa deletar o atributo do settings para simular ausência.
        del settings.B2_CONFIGURED

        with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as mp:
            mp.setenv('B2_KEY_ID', 'x')
            mp.setenv('B2_APPLICATION_KEY', 'y')
            mp.setenv('B2_BUCKET_NAME', 'z')
            mp.setenv('B2_ENDPOINT', 'https://example.com')
            mp.setenv('B2_REGION', 'us-east-005')
            assert BackblazeB2UsageService.is_available() is True

        with pytest.MonkeyPatch.context() as mp:
            mp.delenv('B2_KEY_ID', raising=False)
            assert BackblazeB2UsageService.is_available() is False

        # Restaura para os outros testes não serem afetados
        settings.B2_CONFIGURED = True


# =========================================================================
# FETCH
# =========================================================================

class TestFetch:

    @override_settings(B2_CONFIGURED=False)
    def test_returns_not_ok_when_unavailable(self):
        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is False
        assert result['payload'] is None
        assert 'Credenciais' in result['error']

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_returns_ok_with_empty_bucket(self, mocker):
        paginator = _make_paginator([[]])  # uma página vazia
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is True
        assert result['error'] is None
        payload = result['payload']
        assert payload['total_bytes'] == 0
        assert payload['file_count'] == 0
        assert payload['truncated'] is False
        assert payload['bucket_name'] == 'test-bucket'
        assert payload['payload_version'] == PAYLOAD_VERSION

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_returns_ok_with_single_object(self, mocker):
        paginator = _make_paginator([
            [{'Key': 'a.txt', 'Size': 100}],
        ])
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is True
        assert result['payload']['total_bytes'] == 100
        assert result['payload']['file_count'] == 1

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_returns_ok_with_multiple_pages(self, mocker):
        """Soma correta em múltiplas páginas de paginação."""
        paginator = _make_paginator([
            [
                {'Key': 'a.txt', 'Size': 100},
                {'Key': 'b.txt', 'Size': 250},
            ],
            [
                {'Key': 'c.txt', 'Size': 75},
            ],
        ])
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is True
        assert result['payload']['total_bytes'] == 425
        assert result['payload']['file_count'] == 3

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_ignores_objects_without_size(self, mocker):
        """Objeto sem `Size` (ou não-int) não entra na soma nem na contagem."""
        paginator = _make_paginator([
            [
                {'Key': 'a.txt', 'Size': 100},
                {'Key': 'no-size.txt'},          # sem Size
                {'Key': 'b.txt', 'Size': 'invalid'},  # Size inválido
            ],
        ])
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is True
        assert result['payload']['total_bytes'] == 100
        assert result['payload']['file_count'] == 1

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_handles_client_error(self, mocker):
        paginator = MagicMock()
        paginator.paginate.side_effect = _make_client_error(
            'NoSuchBucket', 'The bucket does not exist'
        )
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is False
        assert result['payload'] is None
        assert 'NoSuchBucket' in result['error']
        assert 'The bucket does not exist' in result['error']

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_handles_endpoint_connection_error(self, mocker):
        paginator = MagicMock()
        paginator.paginate.side_effect = EndpointConnectionError(
            endpoint_url='https://example.com'
        )
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is False
        assert 'inacessível' in result['error'].lower()

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_handles_botocore_error(self, mocker):
        paginator = MagicMock()
        paginator.paginate.side_effect = BotoCoreError()
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is False
        assert 'SDK' in result['error']

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_handles_unexpected_error(self, mocker):
        paginator = MagicMock()
        paginator.paginate.side_effect = ValueError('boom')
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is False
        assert 'ValueError' in result['error']

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_handles_client_creation_failure(self, mocker):
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', side_effect=Exception('no client'))

        result = BackblazeB2UsageService.fetch()

        assert result['ok'] is False
        assert 'no client' in result['error']

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSync:

    @override_settings(B2_CONFIGURED=False)
    def test_creates_unavailable_snapshot(self):
        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_UNAVAILABLE
        assert snapshot.provider_key == PROVIDER_KEY_B2
        assert 'B2 não configurado' in snapshot.error_message
        assert snapshot.storage_usage_bytes is None
        assert snapshot.raw_payload is None

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_creates_ok_snapshot(self, mocker):
        paginator = _make_paginator([
            [
                {'Key': 'a.txt', 'Size': 1000},
                {'Key': 'b.txt', 'Size': 2000},
            ],
        ])
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK
        assert snapshot.provider_key == PROVIDER_KEY_B2
        assert snapshot.storage_usage_bytes == 3000
        assert snapshot.raw_payload['total_bytes'] == 3000
        assert snapshot.raw_payload['file_count'] == 2
        assert snapshot.raw_payload['bucket_name'] == 'test-bucket'
        assert snapshot.raw_payload['payload_version'] == PAYLOAD_VERSION
        assert snapshot.error_message == ''

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_creates_error_snapshot_on_fetch_failure(self, mocker):
        paginator = MagicMock()
        paginator.paginate.side_effect = _make_client_error(
            'AccessDenied', 'Access Denied'
        )
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR
        assert 'AccessDenied' in snapshot.error_message
        assert snapshot.storage_usage_bytes is None
        assert snapshot.raw_payload is None

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_campos_especificos_de_cloudinary_ficam_null(self, mocker):
        """
        Regra: no B2, todos os campos específicos do Cloudinary
        (credits_*, bandwidth_*, transformations_*, plan_name, media_limits,
        rate_limit_*, objects_count, resources_count, derived_resources_count)
        ficam NULL. Nunca preenchidos.
        """
        paginator = _make_paginator([[{'Key': 'a.txt', 'Size': 100}]])
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert snapshot.plan_name == ''
        assert snapshot.storage_credits_usage is None
        assert snapshot.bandwidth_usage_bytes is None
        assert snapshot.bandwidth_credits_usage is None
        assert snapshot.transformations_usage is None
        assert snapshot.transformations_credits_usage is None
        assert snapshot.credits_usage is None
        assert snapshot.credits_limit is None
        assert snapshot.credits_used_percent is None
        assert snapshot.objects_count is None
        assert snapshot.resources_count is None
        assert snapshot.derived_resources_count is None
        assert snapshot.media_limits is None
        assert snapshot.rate_limit_remaining is None
        assert snapshot.rate_limit_reset_at is None

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_sync_never_raises_on_failure(self, mocker):
        """
        Regra fundamental: `sync()` NUNCA levanta exceção. O management
        command precisa terminar com código 0 mesmo em falha da API.
        """
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', side_effect=RuntimeError('anything'))

        # Não deve levantar
        snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()
        assert snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_failure_preserves_previous_ok_snapshot(self, mocker):
        """
        Regra: uma falha NÃO apaga o último snapshot válido.
        Dois snapshots coexistem; a leitura usa o último 'ok'.
        """
        # 1ª chamada: sucesso
        paginator = _make_paginator([[{'Key': 'a.txt', 'Size': 500}]])
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)
        ok_snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        # 2ª chamada: falha
        paginator_fail = MagicMock()
        paginator_fail.paginate.side_effect = RuntimeError('boom')
        client_fail = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator_fail)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client_fail)
        error_snapshot = BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        assert ok_snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK
        assert error_snapshot.status == StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR
        assert ok_snapshot.id != error_snapshot.id

        # Último 'ok' continua existindo
        latest_ok = (
            StorageProviderSnapshot.objects
            .filter(
                provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
                status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            )
            .order_by('-fetched_at')
            .first()
        )
        assert latest_ok is not None
        assert latest_ok.id == ok_snapshot.id

        # O último snapshot (qualquer status) é o de erro
        latest = (
            StorageProviderSnapshot.objects
            .filter(provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2)
            .order_by('-fetched_at')
            .first()
        )
        assert latest.id == error_snapshot.id

    @override_settings(**B2_SETTINGS)
    def test_sync_is_append_only(self, mocker):
        """
        Cada `sync()` cria uma linha NOVA. Nunca sobrescreve.
        Verifica que após N chamadas, existem N snapshots.
        """
        paginator = _make_paginator([[{'Key': 'a.txt', 'Size': 100}]])
        client = _make_client_with_paginator(paginator)
        mocker.patch('boto3.client', return_value=client)

        before = StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.filter(
            provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
        ).count()

        for _ in range(3):
            BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()

        after = StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.filter(
            provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
        ).count()

        assert after == before + 3

"""
Testes da action `change_plan` no AdminWorkspaceViewSet.

Cobre:
    - Auth / autorização.
    - Plano inexistente.
    - Plano inativo (H2).
    - Mesmo plano (H1).
    - Troca com sucesso: encerra antiga, cria nova, preenche auditoria.
    - Sem assinatura ativa: cria nova com reason=manual (H3).
    - Constraint de uma única ativa por workspace.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

from apps.console.models import ConsoleMembership, Plan
from apps.workspaces.models import Workspace, WorkspaceSubscription


User = get_user_model()


def _url(ws):
    return f'/api/console/workspaces/{ws.id}/change-plan/'


@pytest.fixture
def console_admin(db):
    user = User.objects.create_user(
        email='admin-change-plan@example.com',
        password='x',
    )
    ConsoleMembership.objects.create(user=user, created_by=user)
    return user


@pytest.fixture
def admin_client(api_client, console_admin):
    api_client.force_authenticate(user=console_admin)
    return api_client


@pytest.fixture
def plan_pro(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='pro', name='Pro', price=Decimal('99.00'), is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan_basic(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='basic', name='Basic', price=Decimal('49.00'), is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan_inactive(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='old', name='Old', price=Decimal('0.00'), is_active=False,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def workspace(db):
    return Workspace.objects.create(name='WS Change', slug='ws-change')


def _subscribe(workspace, plan, is_active=True):
    return WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
        workspace=workspace, plan=plan, is_active=is_active,
        started_at=workspace.created_at,
    )


# =========================================================================
# AUTENTICAÇÃO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestChangePlanAuth:

    def test_anonymous_gets_401_or_403(self, api_client, workspace, plan_basic):
        response = api_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_basic.key},
            format='json',
        )
        assert response.status_code in (401, 403)


# =========================================================================
# ERROS DE VALIDAÇÃO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestChangePlanValidation:

    def test_missing_plan_key_returns_400(self, admin_client, workspace):
        response = admin_client.post(_url(workspace), {}, format='json')
        assert response.status_code == 400
        assert 'plan_key' in response.data['error']

    def test_unknown_plan_returns_404(self, admin_client, workspace):
        response = admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': 'nonexistent'},
            format='json',
        )
        assert response.status_code == 404

    def test_inactive_plan_returns_400(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_inactive,
    ):
        response = admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_inactive.key},
            format='json',
        )
        assert response.status_code == 400
        assert 'inativo' in response.data['error'].lower()

    def test_same_plan_returns_400(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_pro,
    ):
        _subscribe(workspace, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        response = admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_pro.key},
            format='json',
        )
        assert response.status_code == 400
        assert 'já está no plano' in response.data['error']


# =========================================================================
# TROCA COM SUCESSO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestChangePlanSuccess:

    def test_creates_new_active_subscription(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_pro, plan_basic,
    ):
        old = _subscribe(workspace, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        response = admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_basic.key},
            format='json',
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200
        data = response.data
        assert data['plan_key'] == 'basic'
        assert data['is_active'] is True
        assert data['reason'] == 'manual'
        assert str(data['changed_from_id']) == str(old.id)

        # A antiga foi encerrada
        old.refresh_from_db()
        assert old.is_active is False
        assert old.ended_at is not None

    def test_workspace_without_active_subscription_creates_new(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_basic,
    ):
        response = admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_basic.key},
            format='json',
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['plan_key'] == 'basic'
        assert response.data['changed_from_id'] is None

    def test_only_one_active_subscription_remains(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_pro, plan_basic,
    ):
        _subscribe(workspace, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_basic.key},
            format='json',
        )

        active_count = WorkspaceSubscription.objects.filter(
            workspace=workspace, is_active=True,
        ).count()
        assert active_count == 1

    def test_history_is_preserved(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_pro, plan_basic,
    ):
        _subscribe(workspace, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_basic.key},
            format='json',
        )

        # 2 assinaturas totais: a antiga (inativa) + a nova (ativa)
        assert WorkspaceSubscription.objects.filter(
            workspace=workspace,
        ).count() == 2

    def test_custom_reason_is_persisted(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_pro, plan_basic,
    ):
        _subscribe(workspace, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        response = admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_basic.key, 'reason': 'downgrade'},
            format='json',
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['reason'] == 'downgrade'

    def test_invalid_reason_returns_400(
        self, admin_client, workspace, plan_pro, plan_basic,
    ):
        _subscribe(workspace, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        response = admin_client.post(
            _url(workspace),
            {'plan_key': plan_basic.key, 'reason': 'weird'},
            format='json',
        )
        assert response.status_code == 400

"""
Testes da property `Plan.workspaces_count`.

A property representa "Workspaces com assinatura ATIVA neste plano".
Não conta assinaturas históricas (inativas). Não conta Workspaces
sem assinatura ativa.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest

from apps.console.models import Plan
from apps.workspaces.models import Workspace, WorkspaceSubscription


# =========================================================================
# FIXTURES
# =========================================================================

@pytest.fixture
def plan_a(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='plan-a',
        name='Plan A',
        price=Decimal('10.00'),
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan_b(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='plan-b',
        name='Plan B',
        price=Decimal('20.00'),
        is_active=True,
    )


def _make_workspace(slug: str) -> Workspace:
    return Workspace.objects.create(name=f'WS {slug}', slug=slug)


def _subscribe(workspace, plan, is_active=True):
    return WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
        workspace=workspace,
        plan=plan,
        is_active=is_active,
        started_at=workspace.created_at,
    )


# =========================================================================
# CONTAGEM
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestPlanWorkspacesCount:

    def test_count_zero_for_new_plan(self, plan_a):
        """Plano sem nenhum Workspace associado → 0."""
        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 0

    def test_count_one_active_workspace(self, plan_a):
        """Um Workspace com assinatura ativa → 1."""
        ws = _make_workspace('ws-one')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 1

    def test_count_multiple_active_workspaces(self, plan_a):
        """Vários Workspaces com assinatura ativa → N."""
        ws1 = _make_workspace('ws-multi-1')
        ws2 = _make_workspace('ws-multi-2')
        ws3 = _make_workspace('ws-multi-3')

        _subscribe(ws1, plan_a, is_active=True)
        _subscribe(ws2, plan_a, is_active=True)
        _subscribe(ws3, plan_a, is_active=True)

        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 3

    def test_inactive_subscriptions_do_not_count(self, plan_a):
        """
        Assinatura inativa NÃO incrementa a contagem.

        Cobre tanto o caso "Workspace só tem histórico" quanto
        "Workspace tem histórico + ativa".
        """
        ws_only_inactive = _make_workspace('ws-inactive-only')
        _subscribe(ws_only_inactive, plan_a, is_active=False)

        ws_with_history = _make_workspace('ws-with-history')
        _subscribe(ws_with_history, plan_a, is_active=False)
        _subscribe(ws_with_history, plan_a, is_active=True)

        # Só o segundo Workspace entra (uma assinatura ativa = 1).
        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 1

    def test_workspace_without_any_subscription_not_counted(self, plan_a):
        """
        Workspace sem assinatura ativa (e sem nenhuma assinatura)
        NÃO entra na contagem.
        """
        _make_workspace('ws-no-sub')

        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 0

    def test_workspaces_in_different_plans_counted_separately(
        self, plan_a, plan_b,
    ):
        """Cada plano conta apenas os seus Workspaces ativos."""
        ws_a1 = _make_workspace('ws-a1')
        ws_a2 = _make_workspace('ws-a2')
        ws_b1 = _make_workspace('ws-b1')

        _subscribe(ws_a1, plan_a, is_active=True)
        _subscribe(ws_a2, plan_a, is_active=True)
        _subscribe(ws_b1, plan_b, is_active=True)

        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 2
        assert plan_b.workspaces_count == 1

    def test_count_updates_after_subscription_ends(self, plan_a):
        """
        Quando a assinatura é encerrada (is_active=False), a contagem
        diminui. Isso valida que o filtro é aplicado em tempo real,
        não em cache.
        """
        ws = _make_workspace('ws-end')
        sub = _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 1

        sub.is_active = False
        sub.save(update_fields=['is_active'])

        # Recarrega o plano? Não é necessário — a property dispara
        # query nova a cada chamada, sem cache.
        assert plan_a.workspaces_count == 0

"""
Testes do campo `ProviderContract.included_storage_bytes`.

Contexto:
    O teto operacional de armazenamento do contrato com o Backblaze B2
    é cadastrado MANUALMENTE. A API S3-compatible do B2 não expõe o
    "Daily Storage Cap" da conta; ele só existe no console web do B2,
    em "Caps & Alerts".

    Este teste valida:
        - O campo é aceito como None (teto não cadastrado).
        - O campo é aceito como 0 (teto zero, cadastrado explicitamente).
        - O campo é aceito como inteiro positivo.
        - O campo rejeita valores negativos.
        - `included_storage_display` reflete corretamente cada caso.
        - A API de leitura expõe `included_storage_bytes` e
          `included_storage_display`.
        - A API de escrita aceita atualizar o valor via PATCH.
        - NULL não é confundido com "ilimitado".
"""

from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from rest_framework.test import APIClient

from apps.console.models import (
    ConsoleMembership,
    Provider,
    ProviderContract,
)


User = get_user_model()

PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL = '/api/console/contracts/'


# =========================================================================
# FIXTURES
# =========================================================================

@pytest.fixture
def console_admin(db):
    user = User.objects.create_user(
        email='admin-contract@example.com',
        password='x',
    )
    ConsoleMembership.objects.create(user=user, created_by=user)
    return user


@pytest.fixture
def admin_client(console_admin):
    client = APIClient()
    client.force_authenticate(user=console_admin)
    return client


@pytest.fixture
def provider_b2(db):
    return Provider.objects.create(
        key='backblaze_b2',
        name='Backblaze B2',
        category='storage',
        description='',
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def contract_factory(db, provider_b2):
    def _make(
        name='Clivo Production',
        included_storage_bytes=None,
        is_active=True,
    ):
        return ProviderContract.objects.create(
            provider=provider_b2,
            name=name,
            included_storage_bytes=included_storage_bytes,
            started_at=date(2026, 1, 1),
            is_active=is_active,
        )
    return _make


# =========================================================================
# MODELO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestProviderContractIncludedStorageModel:

    def test_null_is_accepted(self, contract_factory):
        """NULL = teto não cadastrado. Estado válido."""
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=None)
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes is None

    def test_zero_is_accepted(self, contract_factory):
        """Zero = teto cadastrado, explicitamente zerado."""
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=0)
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes == 0

    def test_positive_integer_is_accepted(self, contract_factory):
        """50 GiB expresso em bytes."""
        fifty_gib = 50 * 1024 ** 3
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=fifty_gib)
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes == fifty_gib

    def test_negative_is_rejected_on_save(self, contract_factory):
        """Valor negativo é rejeitado na validação do modelo."""
        from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError

        contract = ProviderContract(
            provider=Provider.objects.get(key='backblaze_b2'),
            name='Bad',
            included_storage_bytes=-1,
            started_at=date(2026, 1, 1),
        )
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            contract.save()

    def test_null_is_not_unlimited(self, contract_factory):
        """
        NULL NÃO significa ilimitado. É apenas ausência de dado.

        Este teste é conceitual: confirma que o campo aceita NULL
        e que, no modelo, não há lógica que converta NULL para
        algum valor sentinela de "infinito".
        """
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=None)
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes is None
        # Nenhum valor derivado deve transformar None em algo
        # que pareça "infinito".
        assert not hasattr(contract, 'is_unlimited_storage')


# =========================================================================
# SERIALIZER DE LEITURA
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestProviderContractIncludedStorageSerializerRead:

    def test_display_null_when_none(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=None)
        resp = admin_client.get(f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/')
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] is None
        assert resp.data['included_storage_display'] is None

    def test_display_zero(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=0)
        resp = admin_client.get(f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/')
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] == 0
        assert resp.data['included_storage_display'] == '0 B'

    def test_display_bytes(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=512)
        resp = admin_client.get(f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/')
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] == 512
        assert resp.data['included_storage_display'] == '512.00 B'

    def test_display_gibibyte(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        fifty_gib = 50 * 1024 ** 3
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=fifty_gib)
        resp = admin_client.get(f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/')
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] == fifty_gib
        assert resp.data['included_storage_display'] == '50.00 GB'


# =========================================================================
# SERIALIZER DE ESCRITA
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestProviderContractIncludedStorageSerializerWrite:

    def test_patch_set_value(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=None)
        fifty_gib = 50 * 1024 ** 3

        resp = admin_client.patch(
            f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/',
            {'included_storage_bytes': fifty_gib},
            format='json',
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes == fifty_gib

    def test_patch_clear_value(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        """
        Enviar `null` explicitamente deve limpar o teto cadastrado.
        Isso é intencional: operador pode decidir que não quer manter
        a informação. Não é o mesmo que "zerar".
        """
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=1024)

        resp = admin_client.patch(
            f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/',
            {'included_storage_bytes': None},
            format='json',
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes is None

    def test_patch_zero(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=1024)

        resp = admin_client.patch(
            f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/',
            {'included_storage_bytes': 0},
            format='json',
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes == 0

    def test_negative_is_rejected(self, admin_client, contract_factory):
        contract = contract_factory(included_storage_bytes=None)

        resp = admin_client.patch(
            f'{PROVIDER_CONTRACTS_URL}{contract.id}/',
            {'included_storage_bytes': -1},
            format='json',
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 400

        # O projeto envelopa erros do DRF em {'error': <string>, 'status_code': int}.
        # O nome do campo aparece dentro da string, não como chave de topo.
        # Asserção tolerante: checa o campo no payload, seja como chave
        # de topo (comportamento padrão do DRF) ou dentro do envelope.
        payload_str = str(resp.data)
        assert 'included_storage_bytes' in payload_str

        # Garante que o valor NÃO foi persistido.
        contract.refresh_from_db()
        assert contract.included_storage_bytes is None

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestHumanizeBytes:

    def test_zero(self):
        from apps.console.api.serializers.provider_serializers import (
            _humanize_bytes,
        )
        assert _humanize_bytes(0) == '0 B'

    def test_bytes(self):
        from apps.console.api.serializers.provider_serializers import (
            _humanize_bytes,
        )
        assert _humanize_bytes(512) == '512.00 B'

    def test_kilobytes(self):
        from apps.console.api.serializers.provider_serializers import (
            _humanize_bytes,
        )
        assert _humanize_bytes(1024) == '1.00 KB'

    def test_megabytes(self):
        from apps.console.api.serializers.provider_serializers import (
            _humanize_bytes,
        )
        assert _humanize_bytes(1024 * 1024) == '1.00 MB'

    def test_gibibyte(self):
        from apps.console.api.serializers.provider_serializers import (
            _humanize_bytes,
        )
        assert _humanize_bytes(1024 ** 3) == '1.00 GB'

    def test_terabyte(self):
        from apps.console.api.serializers.provider_serializers import (
            _humanize_bytes,
        )
        assert _humanize_bytes(1024 ** 4) == '1.00 TB'

"""
Testes do QuotaService.

Cobrem:
    - get_quota_info: resolução via plano, casos ausentes, ambiguidade
    - get_usage_bytes: soma de Document.size + Reference.size
    - get_remaining_bytes: subtração segura, ilimitado, ausente
    - check: máquina de estados completa

Nenhuma chamada de rede. Usa SQLite em memória (pytest-django).

As fixtures de documentos/referências são criadas diretamente via ORM,
porque o QuotaService só olha para `size` — não precisa do ciclo de
upload/storage real.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest

from apps.console.exceptions import AmbiguousQuotaConfigurationError
from apps.console.models import (
    Plan,
    Feature,
    PlanFeature,
    PlanFeatureLimit,
    UsageMetric,
)
from apps.console.services.quota_service import (
    QuotaService,
    QuotaInfo,
    QuotaCheckResult,
    STORAGE_METRIC_KEY,
    WARNING_THRESHOLD,
)
from apps.workspaces.models import Workspace, WorkspaceSubscription


# =========================================================================

        key=STORAGE_METRIC_KEY,
        name='Armazenamento',
        unit='byte',
        kind='gauge',
        origin='derived',
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def other_metric(db):
    return UsageMetric.objects.create(
        key='ai.credits',
        name='Créditos de IA',
        unit='credit',
        kind='counter',
        origin='recorded',
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def feature_storage(db):
    return Feature.objects.create(
        key='armazenamento',
        name='Armazenamento',
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def feature_other(db):
    return Feature.objects.create(
        key='intelligence',
        name='Intelligence',
        is_active=True,
    )

    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='pro',
        name='Pro',
        price=Decimal('99.00'),
        is_active=True,
        is_public=True,

@pytest.fixture

    configurado. Serve para testar o caso 'plano sem limite'.

# =========================================================================
# HELPERS — DOCUMENTOS E REFERENCES
# =========================================================================

def _make_document(workspace, size: int, idx: int = 0):
    """
    Cria um Document mínimo do ponto de vista do QuotaService:
    workspace + size. Não sobe arquivo físico.
    """
    from apps.documents.models import Document
    return Document.objects.create(
        workspace=workspace,
        name=f'doc-{idx}',
        file=f'documents/test-{idx}.bin',  # caminho fictício
        extension='bin',
        mime_type='application/octet-stream',
        size=size,
    )


def _make_reference(workspace, size: int, idx: int = 0):
    """
    Cria uma Reference mínima do ponto de vista do QuotaService:
    workspace + size. Não sobe imagem física.
    """
    from apps.references.models import Reference
    return Reference.objects.create(
        workspace=workspace,
        title=f'ref-{idx}',
        image=f'references/test-{idx}.jpg',
        extension='jpg',
        size=size,
    )


def _set_storage_limit(
    plan, feature, metric,
    limit_value,
    period='monthly',
    behavior='hard_limit',
):
    pf = PlanFeature.objects.create(plan=plan, feature=feature)
    return PlanFeatureLimit.objects.create(
        plan_feature=pf,
        metric=metric,
        limit_value=limit_value,
        period=period,
        behavior=behavior,
    )


# =========================================================================
# GET_QUOTA_INFO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestGetQuotaInfo:

    def test_returns_none_when_no_subscription(self):
        """Workspace sem subscription → None."""
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='Sem plano', slug='sem-plano')
        assert QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws) is None

    def test_returns_none_when_subscription_inactive(
        self, plan_active,
    ):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-inactive')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws,
            plan=plan_active,
            is_active=False,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
            ended_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        assert QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws) is None

    def test_returns_none_when_plan_inactive(
        self, plan_inactive,
    ):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-plan-inactive')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws,
            plan=plan_inactive,
            is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        assert QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws) is None

    def test_returns_none_when_plan_has_no_storage_limit(
        self, plan_active, feature_other, other_metric,
    ):
        # Limite existe, mas é de outra métrica — não deve contar.
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_other, other_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-other-limit')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        assert QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws) is None

    def test_returns_quota_info_when_single_limit_exists(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=5 * 1024 ** 3,
            period='monthly',
            behavior='hard_limit',
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-ok')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )

        info = QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws)

        assert info is not None
        assert isinstance(info, QuotaInfo)
        assert info.limit_value == 5 * 1024 ** 3
        assert info.period == 'monthly'
        assert info.behavior == 'hard_limit'
        assert info.feature_key == 'armazenamento'
        assert info.plan_key == 'pro'

    def test_returns_quota_info_with_unlimited_limit(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=None,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-unlim')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )

        info = QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws)
        assert info is not None
        assert info.limit_value is None

    def test_raises_ambiguous_when_multiple_limits(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, feature_other,
        storage_metric,
    ):
        """
        Mesmo plano, DUAS features diferentes com limite da MESMA métrica.
        Deve levantar AmbiguousQuotaConfigurationError — não somar,
        não escolher.
        """
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_other, storage_metric, 2000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-ambiguous')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )

        with pytest.raises(AmbiguousQuotaConfigurationError) as exc:
            QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws)

        # Mensagem deve citar as features conflitantes
        assert 'armazenamento' in str(exc.value)
        assert 'intelligence' in str(exc.value)

    def test_ignores_inactive_metric(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        """
        Limite cuja métrica está inativa não conta.
        Se for o único, o resultado é None.
        """
        storage_metric.is_active = False
        storage_metric.save(update_fields=['is_active'])

        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-inactive-metric')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )

        assert QuotaService.get_quota_info(ws) is None


# =========================================================================
# GET_USAGE_BYTES
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestGetUsageBytes:

    def test_empty_workspace_returns_zero(self):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-empty')
        assert QuotaService.get_usage_bytes(ws) == 0

    def test_documents_only(self):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-docs')
        _make_document(ws, 100, idx=1)
        _make_document(ws, 250, idx=2)
        assert QuotaService.get_usage_bytes(ws) == 350

    def test_references_only(self):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-refs')
        _make_reference(ws, 500, idx=1)
        _make_reference(ws, 750, idx=2)
        assert QuotaService.get_usage_bytes(ws) == 1250

    def test_documents_plus_references(self):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-both')
        _make_document(ws, 100, idx=1)
        _make_reference(ws, 500, idx=1)
        assert QuotaService.get_usage_bytes(ws) == 600

    def test_isolated_between_workspaces(self):
        ws_a = Workspace.objects.create(name='A', slug='ws-a')
        ws_b = Workspace.objects.create(name='B', slug='ws-b')
        _make_document(ws_a, 1000, idx=1)
        _make_reference(ws_a, 2000, idx=1)
        _make_document(ws_b, 9999, idx=1)

        assert QuotaService.get_usage_bytes(ws_a) == 3000
        assert QuotaService.get_usage_bytes(ws_b) == 9999

    def test_zero_size_documents_are_ignored_in_sum(self):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-zeros')
        _make_document(ws, 0, idx=1)
        _make_document(ws, 0, idx=2)
        _make_reference(ws, 100, idx=1)
        assert QuotaService.get_usage_bytes(ws) == 100


# =========================================================================
# GET_REMAINING_BYTES
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestGetRemainingBytes:

    def test_returns_none_without_quota(self):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-no-quota')
        assert QuotaService.get_remaining_bytes(ws) is None

    def test_returns_none_when_unlimited(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=None,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-unlim-rem')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        assert QuotaService.get_remaining_bytes(ws) is None

    def test_returns_difference(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-diff')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 400, idx=1)

        assert QuotaService.get_remaining_bytes(ws) == 600

    def test_returns_zero_when_used_equals_quota(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-full')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 1000, idx=1)

        assert QuotaService.get_remaining_bytes(ws) == 0

    def test_returns_zero_when_used_exceeds_quota(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-over')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 5000, idx=1)

        assert QuotaService.get_remaining_bytes(ws) == 0

    def test_propagates_ambiguous_error(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, feature_other, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_other, storage_metric, 2000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-amb-rem')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )

        with pytest.raises(AmbiguousQuotaConfigurationError):
            QuotaService.get_remaining_bytes(ws)

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestCheck:

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UNCONFIGURED
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def test_unconfigured_no_plan(self):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-nc-1')
        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert isinstance(result, QuotaCheckResult)
        assert result.status == 'unconfigured'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.quota_bytes is None
        assert result.remaining_bytes is None
        assert result.used_bytes == 0

    def test_unconfigured_plan_without_limit(
        self, plan_active,
    ):
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-nc-2')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'unconfigured'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.quota_bytes is None

    def test_unconfigured_does_not_raise_on_ambiguous(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, feature_other, storage_metric,
    ):
        """
        `check()` NÃO deve levantar — deve retornar 'unconfigured'
        com feature_key='__ambiguous__' para o endpoint administrativo
        detectar e responder 500 se quiser.
        """
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_other, storage_metric, 2000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-nc-amb')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'unconfigured'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.feature_key == '__ambiguous__'

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # UNLIMITED
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def test_unlimited(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=None,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-unl')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 999_999_999, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'unlimited'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.quota_bytes is None
        assert result.remaining_bytes is None
        assert result.used_bytes == 999_999_999

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # OK — uso < 80%
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def test_ok_at_zero_usage(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-ok-zero')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'ok'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.quota_bytes == 1000
        assert result.used_bytes == 0
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 1000
        assert result.percent == 0.0

    def test_ok_below_warning_threshold(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-ok-79')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 790, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'ok'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 210
        assert result.percent == 79.0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # WARNING — 80% ≤ uso < 100%
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def test_warning_at_exactly_80_percent(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-warn-80')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 800, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'warning'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 200
        assert result.percent == 80.0

    def test_warning_just_below_full(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric, 1000,
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-warn-99')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 999, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'warning'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 1

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # BLOCKED — uso ≥ 100% E hard_limit
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def test_blocked_exactly_at_quota_hard_limit(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=1000, behavior='hard_limit',
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-blocked-100')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 1000, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'blocked'
        assert result.allowed is False
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 0
        assert result.percent == 100.0
        assert result.behavior == 'hard_limit'

    def test_blocked_above_quota_hard_limit(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=1000, behavior='hard_limit',
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-blocked-120')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 1200, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'blocked'
        assert result.allowed is False
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 0
        assert result.percent == 120.0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # WARNING acima da quota — soft_limit / overage_allowed
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def test_warning_above_quota_soft_limit(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=1000, behavior='soft_limit',
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-soft-over')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 1200, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'warning'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 0

    def test_warning_above_quota_overage_allowed(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=1000, behavior='overage_allowed',
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-overage')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        _make_document(ws, 1200, idx=1)

        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.status == 'warning'
        assert result.allowed is True
        assert result.remaining_bytes == 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # METADADOS PROPAGADOS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def test_check_propagates_behavior_and_period(
        self, plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_active, feature_storage, storage_metric,
            limit_value=5000,
            period='lifetime',
            behavior='soft_limit',
        )
        ws = Workspace.objects.create(name='WS', slug='ws-meta')
        WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
            workspace=ws, plan=plan_active, is_active=True,
            started_at=ws.created_at,
        )
        result = QuotaService.check(ws)

        assert result.behavior == 'soft_limit'
        assert result.period == 'lifetime'
        assert result.feature_key == 'armazenamento'
        assert result.plan_key == 'pro'

"""
Testes do endpoint administrativo de quota do Console.

Endpoint:
    GET /api/console/quota/workspaces/<workspace_id>/

Requer:
    - autenticação
    - ConsoleMembership (IsConsoleAdmin)
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

from apps.console.models import (
    ConsoleMembership,
    Plan,
    Feature,
    PlanFeature,
    PlanFeatureLimit,
    UsageMetric,
)
from apps.workspaces.models import Workspace, WorkspaceSubscription


User = get_user_model()

QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE = '/api/console/quota/workspaces/{workspace_id}/'


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS
# =========================================================================

@pytest.fixture
def admin_user(db):
    user = User.objects.create_user(
        email='admin-quota@example.com',
        password='x',
    )
    ConsoleMembership.objects.create(user=user, created_by=user)
    return user


@pytest.fixture
def admin_client_quota(api_client, admin_user):
    api_client.force_authenticate(user=admin_user)
    return api_client


@pytest.fixture
def storage_metric(db):
    return UsageMetric.objects.create(
        key='storage.bytes',
        name='Armazenamento',
        unit='byte',
        kind='gauge',
        origin='derived',
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def storage_feature(db):
    return Feature.objects.create(
        key='armazenamento',
        name='Armazenamento',
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='pro',
        name='Pro',
        price=Decimal('99.00'),
        is_active=True,
    )


def _make_workspace(name_suffix):
    return Workspace.objects.create(
        name=f'WS {name_suffix}',
        slug=f'ws-{name_suffix}',
    )


def _subscribe(workspace, plan, is_active=True):
    return WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
        workspace=workspace,
        plan=plan,
        is_active=is_active,
        started_at=workspace.created_at,
    )


def _give_limit(plan, feature, metric, limit_value, behavior='hard_limit'):
    pf = PlanFeature.objects.create(plan=plan, feature=feature)
    PlanFeatureLimit.objects.create(
        plan_feature=pf,
        metric=metric,
        limit_value=limit_value,
        period='monthly',
        behavior=behavior,
    )


def _add_document(workspace, size, idx=0):
    from apps.documents.models import Document
    return Document.objects.create(
        workspace=workspace,
        name=f'doc-{idx}',
        file=f'documents/test-{idx}.bin',
        extension='bin',
        mime_type='application/octet-stream',
        size=size,
    )


# =========================================================================
# AUTENTICAÇÃO / AUTORIZAÇÃO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestQuotaViewAuth:

    def test_anonymous_gets_401_or_403(self, api_client, plan):
        ws = _make_workspace('anon')
        _subscribe(ws, plan)
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(workspace_id=ws.id)
        response = api_client.get(url)
        assert response.status_code in (401, 403)

    def test_non_console_user_gets_403(self, api_client, plan):
        user = User.objects.create_user(email='plain-quota@example.com', password='x')
        api_client.force_authenticate(user=user)
        ws = _make_workspace('plain')
        _subscribe(ws, plan)
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(workspace_id=ws.id)
        response = api_client.get(url)
        assert response.status_code == 403

    def test_console_admin_gets_200(self, admin_client_quota, plan):
        ws = _make_workspace('admin')
        _subscribe(ws, plan)
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(workspace_id=ws.id)
        response = admin_client_quota.get(url)
        assert response.status_code == 200

# WORKSPACE INVÁLIDO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestQuotaViewWorkspaceNotFound:

    def test_inexistent_workspace_returns_404(self, admin_client_quota):
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(
            workspace_id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
        )
        response = admin_client_quota.get(url)
        assert response.status_code == 404

    def test_invalid_uuid_returns_404(self, admin_client_quota):
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(workspace_id='not-a-uuid')
        response = admin_client_quota.get(url)
        assert response.status_code == 404


# =========================================================================
# UNCONFIGURED
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestQuotaViewUnconfigured:

    def test_workspace_without_subscription(
        self, admin_client_quota,
    ):
        ws = _make_workspace('no-sub')
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(workspace_id=ws.id)
        response = admin_client_quota.get(url)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['status'] == 'unconfigured'
        assert response.data['allowed'] is True
        assert response.data['quota_bytes'] is None
        assert response.data['plan_key'] is None
        assert response.data['unconfigured_reason'] == 'workspace sem plano ativo'

    def test_workspace_with_inactive_subscription(
        self, admin_client_quota, plan,
    ):
        ws = _make_workspace('inactive-sub')
        _subscribe(ws, plan, is_active=False)
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(workspace_id=ws.id)
        response = admin_client_quota.get(url)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['status'] == 'unconfigured'
        assert response.data['unconfigured_reason'] == 'assinatura inativa'

    def test_plan_without_storage_limit(
        self, admin_client_quota, plan,
    ):
        ws = _make_workspace('no-limit')
        _subscribe(ws, plan)
        url = QUOTA_URL_TEMPLATE.format(workspace_id=ws.id)
        response = admin_client_quota.get(url)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['status'] == 'unconfigured'
        assert response.data['quota_bytes'] is None
        assert response.data['plan_key'] == 'pro'
        assert response.data['unconfigured_reason'] == (
            'plano sem limite configurado para storage.bytes'
        )

        assert data['plan_name'] == 'Pro'
        assert data['unconfigured_reason'] is None

        assert 'intelligence' in response.data['error']
