"""
Testes do seed `seed_plan_limits`.

Cobrem:
    - Criação em base vazia (com planos ativos).
    - Idempotência: rodar 2x não duplica.
    - Preservação: NÃO sobrescreve PlanFeatureLimit existente.
    - Falha clara quando 'storage.bytes' não existe no catálogo.
    - Não cria nada quando não há plano ativo.
"""

from decimal import Decimal
from io import StringIO

import pytest
from django.core.management import call_command
from django.core.management.base import CommandError

from apps.console.models import (
    Plan,
    Feature,
    PlanFeature,
    PlanFeatureLimit,
    UsageMetric,
)


@pytest.fixture
def storage_metric(db):
    return UsageMetric.objects.create(
        key='storage.bytes',
        name='Armazenamento',
        unit='byte',
        kind='gauge',
        origin='derived',
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan_a(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='starter',
        name='Starter',
        price=Decimal('49.00'),
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan_b(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='pro',
        name='Pro',
        price=Decimal('99.00'),
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan_inactive(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='old',
        name='Old',
        price=Decimal('0.00'),
        is_active=False,
    )


@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSeedPlanLimits:

    def test_fails_without_storage_metric(self):
        Plan.objects.create(
            key='starter', name='Starter',
            price=Decimal('49.00'), is_active=True,
        )
        with pytest.raises(CommandError) as exc:
            call_command('seed_plan_limits')
        assert 'storage.bytes' in str(exc.value)

    def test_creates_feature_and_limits(
        self, storage_metric, plan_a, plan_b,
    ):
        out = StringIO()
        call_command('seed_plan_limits', stdout=out)

        feature = Feature.objects.get(key='armazenamento')
        assert feature.name == 'Armazenamento'
        assert feature.is_active is True

        pf_a = PlanFeature.objects.get(plan=plan_a, feature=feature)
        pf_b = PlanFeature.objects.get(plan=plan_b, feature=feature)

        lim_a = PlanFeatureLimit.objects.get(
            plan_feature=pf_a, metric=storage_metric,
        )
        lim_b = PlanFeatureLimit.objects.get(
            plan_feature=pf_b, metric=storage_metric,
        )

        assert lim_a.limit_value == 5 * 1024 ** 3
        assert lim_a.period == 'monthly'
        assert lim_a.behavior == 'hard_limit'

        assert lim_b.limit_value == 5 * 1024 ** 3
        assert lim_b.behavior == 'hard_limit'

    def test_ignores_inactive_plans(
        self, storage_metric, plan_a, plan_inactive,
    ):
        call_command('seed_plan_limits', stdout=StringIO())

        feature = Feature.objects.get(key='armazenamento')
        # Plano ativo tem PlanFeature
        assert PlanFeature.objects.filter(
            plan=plan_a, feature=feature,
        ).exists()
        # Plano inativo NÃO tem
        assert not PlanFeature.objects.filter(
            plan=plan_inactive, feature=feature,
        ).exists()

    def test_idempotent(self, storage_metric, plan_a):
        call_command('seed_plan_limits', stdout=StringIO())
        call_command('seed_plan_limits', stdout=StringIO())

        assert Feature.objects.filter(key='armazenamento').count() == 1
        feature = Feature.objects.get(key='armazenamento')
        assert PlanFeature.objects.filter(
            plan=plan_a, feature=feature,
        ).count() == 1
        assert PlanFeatureLimit.objects.filter(
            plan_feature__plan=plan_a,
            metric=storage_metric,
        ).count() == 1

    def test_does_not_overwrite_existing_limit(
        self, storage_metric, plan_a,
    ):
        """
        Pré-cria um limite customizado e confirma que o seed
        NÃO sobrescreve.
        """
        feature = Feature.objects.create(
            key='armazenamento',
            name='Armazenamento',
            is_active=True,
        )
        pf = PlanFeature.objects.create(plan=plan_a, feature=feature)
        PlanFeatureLimit.objects.create(
            plan_feature=pf,
            metric=storage_metric,
            limit_value=100,
            period='lifetime',
            behavior='soft_limit',
        )

        call_command('seed_plan_limits', stdout=StringIO())

        lim = PlanFeatureLimit.objects.get(
            plan_feature=pf, metric=storage_metric,
        )
        # Permanece o valor customizado.
        assert lim.limit_value == 100
        assert lim.period == 'lifetime'
        assert lim.behavior == 'soft_limit'

    def test_no_active_plans_does_nothing(
        self, storage_metric, plan_inactive,
    ):
        out = StringIO()
        call_command('seed_plan_limits', stdout=out)

        # Feature 'armazenamento' é criada de qualquer forma.
        assert Feature.objects.filter(key='armazenamento').exists()
        # Mas nenhum PlanFeatureLimit é criado.
        assert PlanFeatureLimit.objects.count() == 0

    def test_reuses_existing_feature(
        self, storage_metric, plan_a,
    ):
        pre = Feature.objects.create(
            key='armazenamento',
            name='Armazenamento (custom)',
            is_active=True,
        )
        call_command('seed_plan_limits', stdout=StringIO())

        # Não duplica.
        assert Feature.objects.filter(key='armazenamento').count() == 1
        # Preserva o registro existente (não atualiza name).
        pre.refresh_from_db()
        assert pre.name == 'Armazenamento (custom)'

"""
Testes do endpoint `/api/console/storage/budget/breakdown/`.

Cobrem:
    - Orçamento ausente → 404 (o breakdown depende do orçamento existir? NÃO)
      → na prática, o breakdown NÃO exige orçamento. Só lê planos e
        workspaces. Testa isso.
    - Sem planos elegíveis → lista vazia, totais zerados.
    - Um plano com quota → linha com quota, workspaces_count, committed.
    - Múltiplos planos → cada linha correta, total somado.
    - Plano sem quota → has_quota=False, quota_bytes=None, committed=0.
    - Plano ilimitado → 409.
    - Configuração ambígua → 409.
    - Autorização (IsConsoleAdmin).
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

from apps.console.models import (
    ConsoleMembership,
    Plan,
    Feature,
    PlanFeature,
    PlanFeatureLimit,
    UsageMetric,
)
from apps.workspaces.models import Workspace, WorkspaceSubscription

    return WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(

        is_active=is_active,
        started_at=ws.created_at,

        metric=metric,
        limit_value=limit_value,
        period='monthly',
        behavior='hard_limit',
    )

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestBreakdownPlans:

    def test_plan_without_workspaces_not_in_breakdown(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_basic,
    ):
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.data['plans'] == []

    def test_plan_with_one_workspace(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_basic,
    ):
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        ws = _make_ws('one')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_basic, is_active=True)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert len(resp.data['plans']) == 1

        row = resp.data['plans'][0]
        assert row['plan_key'] == 'basic'
        assert row['plan_name'] == 'Basic'
        assert row['quota_bytes'] == 5 * GIB
        assert row['has_quota'] is True
        assert row['is_unlimited'] is False
        assert row['workspaces_count'] == 1
        assert row['committed_bytes'] == 5 * GIB

        assert resp.data['totals']['committed_bytes'] == 5 * GIB
        assert resp.data['totals']['workspaces_count'] == 1

    def test_plan_with_three_workspaces(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_basic,
    ):
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        for i in range(3):
            ws = _make_ws(f'w{i}')
            _subscribe(ws, plan_basic, is_active=True)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        row = resp.data['plans'][0]
        assert row['workspaces_count'] == 3
        assert row['committed_bytes'] == 15 * GIB

        assert resp.data['totals']['committed_bytes'] == 15 * GIB
        assert resp.data['totals']['workspaces_count'] == 3

    def test_two_plans(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature,
        plan_basic, plan_pro,
    ):
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        _set_quota(plan_pro, storage_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB)

        ws1 = _make_ws('b1')
        _subscribe(ws1, plan_basic, is_active=True)
        ws2 = _make_ws('b2')
        _subscribe(ws2, plan_basic, is_active=True)
        ws3 = _make_ws('p1')
        _subscribe(ws3, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        assert len(resp.data['plans']) == 2

        by_key = {p['plan_key']: p for p in resp.data['plans']}
        assert by_key['basic']['workspaces_count'] == 2
        assert by_key['basic']['committed_bytes'] == 10 * GIB
        assert by_key['pro']['workspaces_count'] == 1
        assert by_key['pro']['committed_bytes'] == 10 * GIB

        assert resp.data['totals']['committed_bytes'] == 20 * GIB
        assert resp.data['totals']['workspaces_count'] == 3

    def test_plan_without_quota_shows_unconfigured(
        self, admin_client, plan_basic,
    ):
        ws = _make_ws('nq')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_basic, is_active=True)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        row = resp.data['plans'][0]
        assert row['plan_key'] == 'basic'
        assert row['has_quota'] is False
        assert row['quota_bytes'] is None
        assert row['is_unlimited'] is False
        assert row['committed_bytes'] == 0

        assert resp.data['unconfigured_plans_count'] == 1

    def test_inactive_workspace_not_counted(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_basic,
    ):
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        ws_active = _make_ws('active')
        _subscribe(ws_active, plan_basic, is_active=True)
        ws_inactive = Workspace.objects.create(
            name='Inactive', slug='inactive', is_active=False,
        )
        _subscribe(ws_inactive, plan_basic, is_active=True)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        row = resp.data['plans'][0]
        assert row['workspaces_count'] == 1

    def test_inactive_subscription_not_counted(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_basic,
    ):
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        ws = _make_ws('inactive-sub')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_basic, is_active=False)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        assert resp.data['plans'] == []

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestBreakdownErrors:

    def test_unlimited_quota_returns_409(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_basic,
    ):
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, None)
        ws = _make_ws('unl')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_basic, is_active=True)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 409
        assert resp.data['code'] == 'unlimited_quota'

    def test_ambiguous_quota_returns_409(
        self, admin_client, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_basic,
    ):
        from apps.console.models import Feature
        other_feature = Feature.objects.create(
            key='intelligence', name='Intelligence', is_active=True,
        )
        _set_quota(plan_basic, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        _set_quota(plan_basic, other_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB)
        ws = _make_ws('amb')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_basic, is_active=True)

        resp = admin_client.get(BREAKDOWN_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 409
        assert resp.data['code'] == 'ambiguous_quota'

"""
Testes do StorageBudgetService.

Cobrem:
    - Criação via seed (idempotência).
    - Cálculo de allocatable_bytes com reserva.
    - Cálculo de committed_bytes em todos os cenários:
        * Workspace sem assinatura → 0
        * Workspace com assinatura inativa → 0
        * Workspace inativo → 0
        * Plano sem limite → 0
        * Plano com limite → soma
        * Plano ilimitado → bloqueia
        * Configuração ambígua → bloqueia
    - Atualização transacional com select_for_update.
    - Bloqueio de redução abaixo do comprometido (política estrita).
    - Permissão de redução abaixo do comprometido (política controlada).
    - Gravação de histórico com valores anteriores e novos.
"""

from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from django.core.management import call_command
from django.db import IntegrityError, transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from io import StringIO

from apps.console.models import (
    Plan,
    Feature,
    PlanFeature,
    PlanFeatureLimit,
    StorageBudget,
    StorageBudgetChange,
    UsageMetric,
)
from apps.console.services import StorageBudgetService
from apps.console.exceptions import (
    StorageBudgetNotFoundError,
    StorageBudgetBelowCommittedError,
    StorageBudgetUnlimitedQuotaError,
    StorageBudgetAmbiguousQuotaError,
)
from apps.workspaces.models import Workspace, WorkspaceSubscription


GIB = 1024 ** 3

# =========================================================================
# SINGLETON
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSingleton:

    def test_second_budget_via_save_raises_validation_error(self, budget):
        """
        `StorageBudget.save()` chama `full_clean()`, que valida
        `unique=True` do `singleton_key` em Python.

        Portanto, criar um segundo orçamento via `.save()` levanta
        `ValidationError` — o INSERT nem chega a ser emitido.
        """
        from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError

        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            StorageBudget.objects.create(
                singleton_key=StorageBudget.SINGLETON_KEY,
                total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
                reserve_percent=20,
                allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_STRICT,
            )

    def test_second_budget_via_bulk_create_raises_integrity_error(self, budget):
        """
        Prova que a constraint `unique=True` existe NO BANCO.

        `bulk_create()` pula o `full_clean()` do modelo. O INSERT vai
        direto para o banco, e o `IntegrityError` vem do banco, não
        do Python.

        Isso é o invariante real: sob concorrência (dois processos
        tentando criar o singleton ao mesmo tempo), quem garante é a
        constraint do banco, não a validação em Python.
        """
        duplicate = StorageBudget(
            singleton_key=StorageBudget.SINGLETON_KEY,
            total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
            reserve_percent=20,
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_STRICT,
        )

        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            with transaction.atomic():
                StorageBudget.objects.bulk_create([duplicate])


# =========================================================================
# ALLOCATABLE
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestAllocatableBytes:

    def test_20_percent_reserve(self, budget):
        """50 GiB total, 20% reserva → 40 GiB alocável."""
        assert budget.allocatable_bytes == 40 * GIB

    def test_zero_reserve(self):
        b = StorageBudget(
            total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
            reserve_percent=0,
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_STRICT,
        )
        assert b.allocatable_bytes == 100 * GIB

    def test_100_percent_reserve(self):
        b = StorageBudget(
            total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
            reserve_percent=100,
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_STRICT,
        )
        assert b.allocatable_bytes == 0

    def test_rounding_down(self):
        """Cálculo inteiro, arredondando para baixo."""
        b = StorageBudget(
            total_budget_bytes=101,
            reserve_percent=20,
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_STRICT,
        )
        # 101 * 80 // 100 = 80
        assert b.allocatable_bytes == 80

# COMMITTED — cenários

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestCommittedBytes:

    def test_no_workspaces(self, budget):
        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 0

    def test_workspace_without_subscription(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB)
        _make_workspace('no-sub')

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 0

    def test_workspace_with_inactive_subscription(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB)
        ws = _make_workspace('inactive-sub')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=False)

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 0

    def test_inactive_workspace_does_not_count(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB)
        ws = _make_workspace('inactive-ws', is_active=False)
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 0

    def test_plan_without_limit_does_not_count(
        self, budget, plan_a,
    ):
        ws = _make_workspace('no-limit')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 0

    def test_single_workspace_with_limit(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        ws = _make_workspace('one')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 5 * GIB

    def test_multiple_workspaces_same_plan(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        for i in range(3):
            ws = _make_workspace(f'multi-{i}')
            _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 15 * GIB

    def test_different_plans(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a, plan_b,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB)
        _set_storage_limit(plan_b, storage_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB)

        ws_a1 = _make_workspace('a1')
        _subscribe(ws_a1, plan_a, is_active=True)
        ws_a2 = _make_workspace('a2')
        _subscribe(ws_a2, plan_a, is_active=True)
        ws_b1 = _make_workspace('b1')
        _subscribe(ws_b1, plan_b, is_active=True)

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == (
            2 * 5 * GIB + 1 * 10 * GIB
        )

    def test_unlimited_quota_raises(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, None,
        )
        ws = _make_workspace('unlimited')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        with pytest.raises(StorageBudgetUnlimitedQuotaError):
            StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes()

    def test_ambiguous_quota_raises(
        self, budget, storage_metric,
        storage_feature, other_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 5 * GIB,
        )
        _set_storage_limit(
            plan_a, other_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB,
        )
        ws = _make_workspace('ambiguous')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        with pytest.raises(StorageBudgetAmbiguousQuotaError):
            StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes()

    def test_inactive_plan_does_not_count(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature,
    ):
        inactive_plan = Plan.objects.create(
            key='old',
            name='Old',
            price=Decimal('0.00'),
            is_active=False,
        )
        _set_storage_limit(
            inactive_plan, storage_feature, storage_metric, 10 * GIB,
        )
        ws = _make_workspace('inactive-plan')
        _subscribe(ws, inactive_plan, is_active=True)

        assert StorageBudgetService.calculate_committed_bytes() == 0


# =========================================================================
# UPDATE
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestUpdateBudget:

    def test_update_total(self, budget):
        StorageBudgetService.update_budget(
            total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
            reason='Aumento',
        )
        budget.refresh_from_db()
        assert budget.total_budget_bytes == 100 * GIB

    def test_update_reserve(self, budget):
        StorageBudgetService.update_budget(
            reserve_percent=30,
            reason='Aumento de reserva',
        )
        budget.refresh_from_db()
        assert budget.reserve_percent == 30

    def test_update_policy(self, budget):
        StorageBudgetService.update_budget(
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_CONTROLLED,
            reason='Flexibilização',
        )
        budget.refresh_from_db()
        assert budget.allocation_policy == StorageBudget.POLICY_CONTROLLED

    def test_update_raises_when_not_configured(self, db):
        with pytest.raises(StorageBudgetNotFoundError):
            StorageBudgetService.update_budget(total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB)

    def test_update_below_committed_strict_raises(
        self, budget, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 30 * GIB)
        ws = _make_workspace('one')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        # 50 GiB total, 20% reserva → 40 GiB alocável
        # Tentar reduzir total para 30 GiB → 24 GiB alocável < 30 GiB comprometido
        with pytest.raises(StorageBudgetBelowCommittedError):
            StorageBudgetService.update_budget(
                total_budget_bytes=30 * GIB,
                reason='Redução que não cabe',
            )

    def test_update_below_committed_controlled_allows(
        self, storage_metric, storage_feature, plan_a,
    ):
        budget = StorageBudget.objects.create(
            singleton_key=StorageBudget.SINGLETON_KEY,
            total_budget_bytes=50 * GIB,
            reserve_percent=20,
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_CONTROLLED,
        )
        _set_storage_limit(plan_a, storage_feature, storage_metric, 30 * GIB)
        ws = _make_workspace('one')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_a, is_active=True)

        StorageBudgetService.update_budget(
            total_budget_bytes=30 * GIB,
            reason='Sobrealocação intencional',
        )
        budget.refresh_from_db()
        assert budget.total_budget_bytes == 30 * GIB

    def test_update_with_no_change_does_not_create_history(self, budget):
        StorageBudgetService.update_budget(
            total_budget_bytes=budget.total_budget_bytes,
            reserve_percent=budget.reserve_percent,
        )
        assert StorageBudgetChange.objects.filter(budget=budget).count() == 0

    def test_update_writes_history(
        self, budget, django_user_model,
    ):
        user = django_user_model.objects.create_user(
            email='admin-budget@example.com',
            password='x',
        )

        StorageBudgetService.update_budget(
            total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
            reserve_percent=30,
            reason='Duplicando orçamento',
            changed_by=user,
        )

        changes = StorageBudgetChange.objects.filter(budget=budget)
        assert changes.count() == 1
        change = changes.first()
        assert change.previous_total_bytes == 50 * GIB
        assert change.new_total_bytes == 100 * GIB
        assert change.previous_reserve_percent == 20
        assert change.new_reserve_percent == 30
        assert change.changed_by == user
        assert change.reason == 'Duplicando orçamento'


# =========================================================================
# SEED
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSeedStorageBudget:

    def test_seed_creates_budget(self):
        call_command('seed_storage_budget', stdout=StringIO())
        budget = StorageBudget.objects.first()
        assert budget is not None
        assert budget.total_budget_bytes == 50 * GIB
        assert budget.reserve_percent == 20
        assert budget.allocation_policy == StorageBudget.POLICY_STRICT

    def test_seed_is_idempotent(self):
        call_command('seed_storage_budget', stdout=StringIO())
        call_command('seed_storage_budget', stdout=StringIO())
        assert StorageBudget.objects.count() == 1

    def test_seed_does_not_overwrite_existing(self):
        StorageBudget.objects.create(
            singleton_key=StorageBudget.SINGLETON_KEY,
            total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
            reserve_percent=10,
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_CONTROLLED,
        )
        call_command('seed_storage_budget', stdout=StringIO())
        budget = StorageBudget.objects.first()
        assert budget.total_budget_bytes == 100 * GIB
        assert budget.reserve_percent == 10
        assert budget.allocation_policy == StorageBudget.POLICY_CONTROLLED

    def test_seed_force_overwrites(self):
        StorageBudget.objects.create(
            singleton_key=StorageBudget.SINGLETON_KEY,
            total_budget_bytes=100 * GIB,
            reserve_percent=10,
            allocation_policy=StorageBudget.POLICY_CONTROLLED,
        )
        call_command(
            'seed_storage_budget',
            '--force',
            '--total', '200GiB',
            '--reserve', '25',
            '--policy', 'strict',
            stdout=StringIO(),
        )
        budget = StorageBudget.objects.first()
        assert budget.total_budget_bytes == 200 * GIB
        assert budget.reserve_percent == 25
        assert budget.allocation_policy == StorageBudget.POLICY_STRICT

    def test_seed_creates_history(self):
        call_command('seed_storage_budget', stdout=StringIO())
        assert StorageBudgetChange.objects.count() == 1
        change = StorageBudgetChange.objects.first()
        assert change.new_total_bytes == 50 * GIB
        assert 'seed' in change.reason.lower()

"""
Testes do endpoint `/api/console/storage/provider-cap/`.

Cobrem:
    - Provider inexistente → payload com campos nulos.
    - Provider existe sem contrato → is_configured=False.
    - Provider existe com contrato sem teto → is_configured=False.
    - Provider existe com contrato com teto → is_configured=True.
    - Snapshot 'ok' presente → usage_bytes preenchido.
    - Snapshot 'ok' ausente → usage_bytes=None.
    - Último snapshot com erro → last_snapshot_status/error.
    - Autorização.
"""

from datetime import date

import pytest
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.console.models import (
    ConsoleMembership,
    Provider,
    ProviderContract,
    StorageProviderSnapshot,
)


User = get_user_model()

CAP_URL = '/api/console/storage/provider-cap/'

GIB = 1024 ** 3


# =========================================================================
# FIXTURES
# =========================================================================

@pytest.fixture
def admin_client(db):
    from rest_framework.test import APIClient
    user = User.objects.create_user(
        email='admin-cap@example.com',
        password='x',
    )
    ConsoleMembership.objects.create(user=user, created_by=user)
    client = APIClient()
    client.force_authenticate(user=user)
    return client


@pytest.fixture
def plain_client(db):
    from rest_framework.test import APIClient
    user = User.objects.create_user(
        email='plain-cap@example.com',
        password='x',
    )
    client = APIClient()
    client.force_authenticate(user=user)
    return client


@pytest.fixture
def b2_provider(db):
    return Provider.objects.create(
        key='backblaze_b2',
        name='Backblaze B2',
        category='storage',
        is_active=True,
    )


def _make_contract(provider, included_bytes=None, is_active=True):
    return ProviderContract.objects.create(
        provider=provider,
        name='Production',
        included_storage_bytes=included_bytes,
        started_at=date(2026, 1, 1),
        is_active=is_active,
    )


def _make_snapshot(status='ok', usage_bytes=None, provider_key='backblaze_b2'):
    return StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.create(
        provider_key=provider_key,
        status=status,
        storage_usage_bytes=usage_bytes,
        fetched_at=timezone.now(),
    )

    def test_anonymous_gets_401_or_403(self, api_client):

        client = APIClient()
        resp = client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.status_code in (401, 403)

    def test_non_console_user_gets_403(self, plain_client):
        resp = plain_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 403

    def test_admin_gets_200(self, admin_client):
        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 200

        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] is None
        assert resp.data['is_configured'] is False

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestProviderCapContractWithoutCap:

    def test_contract_without_cap_not_configured(
        self, admin_client, b2_provider,
    ):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=None)

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.data['contract_id'] is not None
        assert resp.data['contract_name'] == 'Production'
        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] is None
        assert resp.data['is_configured'] is False


# =========================================================================
# CONTRATO COM TETO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestProviderCapContractWithCap:

    def test_contract_with_cap_configured(
        self, admin_client, b2_provider,
    ):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=50 * GIB)

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] == 50 * GIB
        assert resp.data['is_configured'] is True

    def test_inactive_contract_ignored(self, admin_client, b2_provider):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=50 * GIB, is_active=False)

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.data['contract_id'] is None
        assert resp.data['is_configured'] is False

    def test_multiple_active_contracts_picks_most_recent(
        self, admin_client, b2_provider,
    ):
        old = ProviderContract.objects.create(
            provider=b2_provider,
            name='Old',
            included_storage_bytes=10 * GIB,
            started_at=date(2025, 1, 1),
            is_active=True,
        )
        new = ProviderContract.objects.create(
            provider=b2_provider,
            name='New',
            included_storage_bytes=50 * GIB,
            started_at=date(2026, 1, 1),
            is_active=True,
        )

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)

        # O serializer serializa `contract_id` como string no payload
        # JSON. Compara convertendo o `new.id` (UUID) para string.
        assert resp.data['contract_id'] == str(new.id)
        assert resp.data['included_storage_bytes'] == 50 * GIB

        # Sanidade: `old` foi criado e existe, mas não é o escolhido.
        assert old.is_active is True
        assert old.id != new.id


# =========================================================================
# SNAPSHOT
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestProviderCapSnapshot:

    def test_ok_snapshot_provides_usage(
        self, admin_client, b2_provider,
    ):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=50 * GIB)
        _make_snapshot(status='ok', usage_bytes=42 * GIB)

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.data['usage_bytes'] == 42 * GIB
        assert resp.data['snapshot_status'] == 'ok'
        assert resp.data['last_snapshot_status'] == 'ok'

    def test_no_snapshot_usage_null(self, admin_client, b2_provider):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=50 * GIB)

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.data['usage_bytes'] is None
        assert resp.data['snapshot_status'] == ''
        assert resp.data['last_snapshot_status'] == ''

    def test_error_snapshot_does_not_provide_usage(
        self, admin_client, b2_provider,
    ):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=50 * GIB)
        _make_snapshot(status='error', usage_bytes=None)

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.data['usage_bytes'] is None
        assert resp.data['last_snapshot_status'] == 'error'

    def test_error_after_ok_keeps_last_usage(
        self, admin_client, b2_provider,
    ):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=50 * GIB)

        # Snapshot 'ok' com uso.
        StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.create(
            provider_key='backblaze_b2',
            status='ok',
            storage_usage_bytes=42 * GIB,
            fetched_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        # Snapshot de erro mais recente.
        StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.create(
            provider_key='backblaze_b2',
            status='error',
            error_message='Falha temporária',
            fetched_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        # Uso físico ainda é o do 'ok' mais recente.
        assert resp.data['usage_bytes'] == 42 * GIB
        # Último status é o erro.
        assert resp.data['last_snapshot_status'] == 'error'
        assert resp.data['last_snapshot_error'] == 'Falha temporária'

    def test_other_provider_snapshot_ignored(
        self, admin_client, b2_provider,
    ):
        _make_contract(b2_provider, included_bytes=50 * GIB)
        _make_snapshot(
            status='ok',
            usage_bytes=1 * GIB,
            provider_key='outro_provider',
        )

        resp = admin_client.get(CAP_URL)
        assert resp.data['usage_bytes'] is None

"""
Testes dos endpoints do StorageViewSet.

Cobre:
    - Autenticação e autorização (IsAuthenticated + IsConsoleAdmin).
    - Envelope `current` / `latest`.
    - Casos: nunca sincronizado, só ok, erro após ok.
    - `is_stale` e `is_failed` derivados.
    - Histórico com paginação limitada.
    - Isolamento entre provider_key diferentes.

Usa as fixtures `admin_client` / `plain_client` / `api_client` definidas
em `apps/console/tests/conftest.py`.
"""

from datetime import timedelta

import pytest
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.console.models import StorageProviderSnapshot
from apps.console.services.b2_usage_service import PROVIDER_KEY_B2


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS
# =========================================================================

SNAPSHOT_URL = '/api/console/storage/provider-snapshot/'
HISTORY_URL = '/api/console/storage/provider-snapshot/history/'


def _make_snapshot(
    status: str = StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
    provider_key: str = PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
    storage_usage_bytes: int = 100,
    raw_payload: dict = None,
    error_message: str = '',
    fetched_at=None,
):
    """Cria um snapshot real no banco de teste."""
    return StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.create(
        provider_key=provider_key,
        status=status,
        storage_usage_bytes=storage_usage_bytes,
        raw_payload=raw_payload,
        error_message=error_message,
        fetched_at=fetched_at or timezone.now(),
    )


# =========================================================================
# AUTENTICAÇÃO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestStorageViewAuth:

    def test_anonymous_gets_401_or_403(self, api_client):
        response = api_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.status_code in (401, 403)

    def test_authenticated_without_console_membership_gets_403(self, plain_client):
        response = plain_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 403

    def test_history_requires_console_membership(self, plain_client):
        response = plain_client.get(HISTORY_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 403

    def test_admin_gets_200(self, admin_client):
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 200


# =========================================================================

# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSnapshotEmpty:

    def test_both_null_when_no_snapshots(self, admin_client):
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['current'] is None
        assert response.data['latest'] is None


# =========================================================================
# ENVELOPE current / latest — SÓ OK
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSnapshotOnlyOk:

    def test_current_equals_latest(self, admin_client):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=2048,
        )

        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['current'] is not None
        assert response.data['latest'] is not None
        assert response.data['current']['id'] == str(snapshot.id)
        assert response.data['latest']['id'] == str(snapshot.id)

    def test_is_failed_false_for_ok(self, admin_client):
        _make_snapshot(status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK)
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.data['current']['is_failed'] is False

    def test_is_stale_false_for_recent_ok(self, admin_client):
        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            fetched_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.data['current']['is_stale'] is False


# =========================================================================
# ENVELOPE current / latest — ERRO APÓS OK
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestSnapshotErrorAfterOk:

    def test_current_is_last_ok_while_latest_is_error(self, admin_client):
        # Snapshot 'ok' antigo
        ok_snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=5000,
            fetched_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=2),
        )
        # Snapshot 'error' mais recente
        error_snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR,
            error_message='falha temporária',
            fetched_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)

        assert response.data['current']['id'] == str(ok_snapshot.id)
        assert response.data['latest']['id'] == str(error_snapshot.id)

    def test_current_is_not_failed(self, admin_client):
        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            fetched_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=2),
        )
        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR,
            error_message='boom',
            fetched_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)

        assert response.data['current']['is_failed'] is False
        assert response.data['latest']['is_failed'] is True

    def test_error_snapshot_is_not_stale(self, admin_client):
        """
        Regra: só snapshots 'ok' podem ser stale. Um snapshot de erro
        é 'failed', o que é outro estado — não conta como stale.
        """
        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR,
            error_message='boom',
            fetched_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(days=5),
        )

        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)

        assert response.data['latest']['is_stale'] is False
        assert response.data['latest']['is_failed'] is True


# =========================================================================
# IS_STALE
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestStale:

    def test_ok_within_25h_not_stale(self, admin_client):
        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            fetched_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=23),
        )
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.data['current']['is_stale'] is False

    def test_ok_beyond_25h_is_stale(self, admin_client):
        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            fetched_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=26),
        )
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.data['current']['is_stale'] is True

    def test_stale_uses_setting_override(self, admin_client, settings):
        """
        O limiar é configurável via `STORAGE_SNAPSHOT_STALE_HOURS`.
        Aqui sobrescrevemos para 1h e validamos que um snapshot de
        2h é considerado stale.
        """
        settings.STORAGE_SNAPSHOT_STALE_HOURS = 1

        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            fetched_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=2),
        )
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)
        assert response.data['current']['is_stale'] is True


# =========================================================================
# ISOLAMENTO ENTRE PROVIDERS
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestProviderIsolation:

    def test_other_provider_snapshot_is_ignored(self, admin_client):
        """
        Um snapshot com provider_key='outro_provider' NÃO aparece
        no endpoint — porque o default é 'backblaze_b2'.
        """
        _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            provider_key='outro_provider',
            storage_usage_bytes=999999,
        )
        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)

        assert response.data['current'] is None
        assert response.data['latest'] is None

    def test_default_provider_key_is_b2(self, admin_client):
        b2_snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
            storage_usage_bytes=1234,
        )

        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL)

        assert response.data['current']['id'] == str(b2_snapshot.id)

    def test_explicit_provider_key_query_param(self, admin_client):
        other_snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            provider_key='outro_provider',
            storage_usage_bytes=5000,
        )

        response = admin_client.get(SNAPSHOT_URL + '?provider_key=outro_provider')

        assert response.data['current']['id'] == str(other_snapshot.id)


# =========================================================================
# HISTORY
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestHistory:

    def test_empty_history(self, admin_client):
        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['count'] == 0
        assert response.data['results'] == []
        assert response.data['provider_key'] == PROVIDER_KEY_B2

    def test_history_default_limit_20(self, admin_client):
        for _ in range(25):
            _make_snapshot(status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK)

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL)

        assert response.data['count'] == 20

    def test_history_custom_limit(self, admin_client):
        for _ in range(10):
            _make_snapshot(status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK)

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL + '?limit=3')

        assert response.data['count'] == 3

    def test_history_limit_capped_at_100(self, admin_client):
        for _ in range(5):
            _make_snapshot(status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK)

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL + '?limit=999')

        # Só existem 5; o limite máximo é 100, mas a resposta é min(5, 100)
        assert response.data['count'] == 5

    def test_history_limit_invalid_falls_back_to_20(self, admin_client):
        for _ in range(25):
            _make_snapshot(status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK)

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL + '?limit=abc')

        assert response.data['count'] == 20

    def test_history_limit_zero_falls_back_to_1(self, admin_client):
        for _ in range(5):
            _make_snapshot(status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK)

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL + '?limit=0')

        # O clamp inferior é 1
        assert response.data['count'] == 1

    def test_history_limit_negative_falls_back_to_1(self, admin_client):
        for _ in range(5):
            _make_snapshot(status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK)

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL + '?limit=-3')

        assert response.data['count'] == 1

    def test_history_isolates_providers(self, admin_client):
        for _ in range(3):
            _make_snapshot(
                status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
                provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
            )
        for _ in range(5):
            _make_snapshot(
                status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
                provider_key='outro_provider',
            )

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL)

        # Só os 3 do B2
        assert response.data['count'] == 3

    def test_history_ordering_newest_first(self, admin_client):
        old = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=100,
            fetched_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=5),
        )
        new = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=200,
            fetched_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        response = admin_client.get(HISTORY_URL)

        results = response.data['results']
        assert results[0]['id'] == str(new.id)
        assert results[1]['id'] == str(old.id)

"""
Testes do management command `sync_b2_usage`.

O command é uma camada fina sobre `BackblazeB2UsageService.sync()`.
Por isso, aqui mockamos `sync()` — não a API do B2. Os testes da API
real estão em `test_b2_usage_service.py` (mock do boto3) e no teste
de integração opt-in.

Estes testes cobrem apenas o comportamento do command:
    - escolha do formato de saída (humano / JSON / quiet)
    - exit code 0 mesmo em falha
    - conteúdo do JSON
    - texto humano para cada status
"""

import json
from io import StringIO
from unittest.mock import patch

import pytest
from django.core.management import call_command
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.console.models import StorageProviderSnapshot
from apps.console.services.b2_usage_service import PROVIDER_KEY_B2


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS
# =========================================================================

def _make_snapshot(
    status: str = StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
    storage_usage_bytes: int = None,
    raw_payload: dict = None,
    error_message: str = '',
):
    """
    Cria um StorageProviderSnapshot real no banco, para o command
    consumir via `sync()` mockado.

    Não usa mock do modelo — o snapshot é real e é persistido.
    """
    return StorageProviderSnapshot.objects.create(
        provider_key=PROVIDER_KEY_B2,
        status=status,
        raw_payload=raw_payload,
        storage_usage_bytes=storage_usage_bytes,
        error_message=error_message,
        fetched_at=timezone.now(),
    )


# =========================================================================
# OUTPUT HUMANO — SUCESSO
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestCommandHumanOutput:

    def test_success_shows_b2_sincronizado(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=1024,
            raw_payload={
                'bucket_name': 'test-bucket',
                'file_count': 3,
                'total_bytes': 1024,
                'truncated': False,
            },
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', stdout=out)

        output = out.getvalue()
        assert 'B2 sincronizado' in output
        assert 'test-bucket' in output
        assert '1024' in output
        assert '3' in output  # file_count

    def test_success_shows_snapshot_id(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=500,
            raw_payload={'bucket_name': 'b', 'file_count': 1, 'total_bytes': 500},
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', stdout=out)

        assert str(snapshot.id) in out.getvalue()

    def test_success_with_truncated_warns(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=100,
            raw_payload={
                'bucket_name': 'b',
                'file_count': 1,
                'total_bytes': 100,
                'truncated': True,
            },
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', stdout=out)

        assert 'Paginação interrompida' in out.getvalue()

            error_message='B2 ClientError NoSuchBucket: bucket does not exist',
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', stdout=out)

        output = out.getvalue()
        assert 'Falha ao consultar o B2' in output
        assert 'NoSuchBucket' in output

    def test_error_does_not_raise(self):
        """
        Regra fundamental: o command NUNCA levanta exceção para o
        chamador, para não poluir logs de cron. Exit code sempre 0.
        """
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR,
            error_message='boom',
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            # Não deve levantar SystemExit nem nada
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', stdout=out)

    def test_error_human_mentions_previous_snapshot(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR,
            error_message='fail',
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', stdout=out)

        assert 'snapshot anterior' in out.getvalue().lower()

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestCommandJson:

    def test_json_output_is_valid(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=1024,
            raw_payload={
                'bucket_name': 'test-bucket',
                'file_count': 3,
                'total_bytes': 1024,
                'truncated': False,
            },
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', '--json', stdout=out)

        data = json.loads(out.getvalue())

        assert data['status'] == 'ok'
        assert data['provider_key'] == PROVIDER_KEY_B2
        assert data['storage_usage_bytes'] == 1024
        assert data['bucket_name'] == 'test-bucket'
        assert data['file_count'] == 3
        assert data['truncated'] is False
        assert data['error_message'] == ''
        assert data['id'] == str(snapshot.id)
        assert 'fetched_at' in data

    def test_json_output_on_error(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_ERROR,
            error_message='AccessDenied',
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', '--json', stdout=out)

        data = json.loads(out.getvalue())

        assert data['status'] == 'error'
        assert data['error_message'] == 'AccessDenied'
        assert data['storage_usage_bytes'] is None
        assert data['bucket_name'] is None
        assert data['file_count'] is None
        assert data['truncated'] is None

    def test_json_output_on_unavailable(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_UNAVAILABLE,
            error_message='B2 não configurado',
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', '--json', stdout=out)

        data = json.loads(out.getvalue())
        assert data['status'] == 'unavailable'
        assert 'B2 não configurado' in data['error_message']

    def test_json_with_truncated_payload(self):
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=999,
            raw_payload={
                'bucket_name': 'b',
                'file_count': 2,
                'total_bytes': 999,
                'truncated': True,
            },
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', '--json', stdout=out)

        data = json.loads(out.getvalue())
        assert data['truncated'] is True


# =========================================================================
# PRIORIDADE ENTRE FLAGS
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestCommandFlagPriority:

    def test_json_wins_over_quiet(self):
        """
        Regra do command: se `--json`, imprime JSON — mesmo com
        `--quiet` presente. `--json` é formato explícito; `--quiet`
        é para cron silencioso. `--json` tem prioridade.
        """
        snapshot = _make_snapshot(
            status=StorageProviderSnapshot.STATUS_OK,
            storage_usage_bytes=1,
            raw_payload={'bucket_name': 'b', 'file_count': 1, 'total_bytes': 1},
        )

        with patch(
            'apps.console.management.commands.sync_b2_usage.'
            'BackblazeB2UsageService.sync',
            return_value=snapshot,
        ):
            out = StringIO()
            call_command('sync_b2_usage', '--quiet', '--json', stdout=out)

        output = out.getvalue()
        # Deve ser JSON válido, não vazio
        data = json.loads(output)
        assert data['status'] == 'ok'

"""
Testes do AdminWorkspaceViewSet.

Cobrem:
    - Autenticação e autorização (IsAuthenticated + IsConsoleAdmin).
    - Listagem com filtros (q, is_active, city).
    - Contagem de queries constante — sem N+1 por assinatura.

O endpoint é paginado pelo DRF (DEFAULT_PAGINATION_CLASS global).
Por isso `response.data` é o envelope `{'count', 'next', 'previous',
'results'}`, não a lista direta. Os testes usam `_results(response)`
para extrair a lista.

O teste de query-count mede o comportamento real do serializer. Ele
flagra N+1 — inclusive os que NÃO são de assinatura (members_count,
governance_keys). O limiar de `assertNumQueries` foi escolhido para
acomodar os N+1 pré-existentes do serializer, mas garantir que o
N+1 de assinatura NÃO cresça com o número de workspaces.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

from apps.console.models import ConsoleMembership, Plan
from apps.workspaces.models import Workspace, WorkspaceSubscription


User = get_user_model()

LIST_URL = '/api/console/workspaces/'


# =========================================================================
# HELPERS
# =========================================================================

def _results(response):
    """
    Extrai a lista de resultados do envelope paginado do DRF.

    O `AdminWorkspaceViewSet` é paginado (DEFAULT_PAGINATION_CLASS
    global). `response.data` é o dicionário
    `{'count', 'next', 'previous', 'results'}`, não a lista.
    """
    return response.data['results']


def _make_workspace(slug: str, name: str = None, city: str = '') -> Workspace:
    return Workspace.objects.create(
        name=name or f'WS {slug}',
        slug=slug,
        city=city,
    )


def _subscribe(workspace, plan, is_active=True):
    return WorkspaceSubscription.objects.create(
        workspace=workspace,
        plan=plan,
        is_active=is_active,
        started_at=workspace.created_at,
    )


# =========================================================================
# FIXTURES
# =========================================================================

@pytest.fixture
def plain_user_admin(db):
    """Usuário ativo, SEM ConsoleMembership."""
    return User.objects.create_user(
        email='plain-admin-ws@example.com',
        password='senha-fraca-123',
    )


@pytest.fixture
def console_admin_user(db):
    """Usuário ativo COM ConsoleMembership."""
    user = User.objects.create_user(
        email='admin-ws@example.com',
        password='senha-fraca-123',
    )
    ConsoleMembership.objects.create(user=user, created_by=user)
    return user


@pytest.fixture
def admin_client(api_client, console_admin_user):
    api_client.force_authenticate(user=console_admin_user)
    return api_client


@pytest.fixture
def plain_client(api_client, plain_user_admin):
    api_client.force_authenticate(user=plain_user_admin)
    return api_client


@pytest.fixture
def plan_pro(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='pro',
        name='Pro',
        price=Decimal('99.00'),
        is_active=True,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def plan_basic(db):
    return Plan.objects.create(
        key='basic',
        name='Basic',
        price=Decimal('49.00'),
        is_active=True,
    )

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestAdminWorkspaceViewAuth:

    def test_anonymous_gets_401_or_403(self, api_client):
        response = api_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code in (401, 403)

    def test_authenticated_without_console_membership_gets_403(
        self, plain_client,
    ):
        response = plain_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 403

    def test_admin_gets_200(self, admin_client):
        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 200


# =========================================================================
# LISTAGEM BÁSICA
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestAdminWorkspaceViewListing:

    def test_empty_list(self, admin_client):
        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['count'] == 0
        assert _results(response) == []

    def test_lists_all_workspaces(self, admin_client, plan_pro):
        ws1 = _make_workspace('ws-1')
        ws2 = _make_workspace('ws-2')
        _subscribe(ws1, plan_pro, is_active=True)
        # ws2 sem assinatura

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['count'] == 2
        assert len(_results(response)) == 2

    def test_serializer_exposes_plan_of_active_subscription(
        self, admin_client, plan_pro,
    ):
        ws = _make_workspace('ws-plan')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['plan_key'] == 'pro'
        assert results[0]['plan_name'] == 'Pro'

    def test_workspace_without_active_subscription_has_null_plan(
        self, admin_client, plan_pro,
    ):
        ws = _make_workspace('ws-no-plan')
        # Apenas assinatura inativa — não conta como plano ativo.
        _subscribe(ws, plan_pro, is_active=False)

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['plan_key'] is None
        assert results[0]['plan_name'] is None

    def test_plan_ignores_historical_inactive_subscriptions(
        self, admin_client, plan_pro, plan_basic,
    ):
        """
        Workspace com histórico de dois planos: só a ativa aparece.
        """
        ws = _make_workspace('ws-history')
        _subscribe(ws, plan_basic, is_active=False)
        _subscribe(ws, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['plan_key'] == 'pro'


# =========================================================================
# FILTROS
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestAdminWorkspaceViewFilters:

    def test_filter_by_q_matches_name(self, admin_client):
        _make_workspace('ws-alpha', name='Alpha Studio')
        _make_workspace('ws-beta', name='Beta Studio')

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL + '?q=alpha')
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['name'] == 'Alpha Studio'

    def test_filter_by_q_matches_slug(self, admin_client):
        _make_workspace('ws-gamma', name='Outro Nome')
        _make_workspace('ws-other', name='Nome Diferente')

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL + '?q=gamma')
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['slug'] == 'ws-gamma'

    def test_filter_by_is_active(self, admin_client):
        active_ws = _make_workspace('ws-active')
        inactive_ws = _make_workspace('ws-inactive')
        inactive_ws.is_active = False
        inactive_ws.save(update_fields=['is_active'])

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL + '?is_active=1')
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['slug'] == 'ws-active'

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL + '?is_active=0')
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['slug'] == 'ws-inactive'

    def test_filter_by_city(self, admin_client):
        _make_workspace('ws-salvador', city='Salvador')
        _make_workspace('ws-saopaulo', city='São Paulo')

        response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL + '?city=salvador')
        assert response.status_code == 200
        results = _results(response)
        assert len(results) == 1
        assert results[0]['city'] == 'Salvador'


# =========================================================================
# QUERY COUNT — SEM N+1 DE ASSINATURA
# =========================================================================

@pytest.mark.django_db
class TestAdminWorkspaceViewQueryCount:
    """
    O que este teste mede:

        O N+1 de ASSINATURA não existe. O `Prefetch` filtrado popula
        `_active_subscriptions`, e a property `Workspace.active_subscription`
        consome esse cache.

    O que este teste NÃO mede (ainda existem, fora do escopo do B2):

        O serializer `AdminWorkspaceSerializer` tem outros dois N+1:
            - `get_members_count`: 1 query por workspace
            - `get_governance_keys`: 1 query por workspace

        Esses N+1 pré-existentes fazem o número total de queries
        crescer linearmente com o número de workspaces — mas por
        razões que NÃO são assinatura.

    Por isso a asserção usa `assertNumQueries(..., exact=False)`:
    mede um TETO. O importante é que o teto seja o mesmo para 5 e
    para 10 workspaces — se o N+1 de assinatura voltasse, esse teto
    seria estourado.
    """

    def test_listing_with_many_workspaces_is_bounded(
        self, admin_client, plan_pro, django_assert_num_queries,
    ):
        """
        Cria 10 Workspaces, todos com assinatura ativa.

        Conta esperada:
            - 1 auth (ConsoleMembership)
            - 1 COUNT (paginação)
            - 1 SELECT workspace
            - 1 Prefetch de assinaturas ativas
            - 20 queries do serializer (2 por workspace: members + governance)
            - ~algumas queries de sessão/middleware
        """
        for i in range(10):
            ws = _make_workspace(f'ws-many-{i}')
            _subscribe(ws, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        # Teto: o N+1 de assinatura NÃO conta. Se contasse, seria
        # 10 queries a mais (uma por workspace). O teto reflete apenas
        # os N+1 pré-existentes do serializer.
        with django_assert_num_queries(30, exact=False):
            response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['count'] == 10
        assert len(_results(response)) == 10

    def test_listing_with_mixed_workspaces_is_bounded(
        self, admin_client, plan_pro, django_assert_num_queries,
    ):
        """
        Mistura: Workspaces com assinatura ativa, com apenas histórico,
        e sem nenhuma assinatura. O número de queries deve permanecer
        dentro do mesmo teto — o Prefetch de assinatura não depende
        da presença delas.
        """
        for i in range(5):
            ws = _make_workspace(f'ws-active-{i}')
            _subscribe(ws, plan_pro, is_active=True)

        for i in range(3):
            ws = _make_workspace(f'ws-hist-{i}')
            _subscribe(ws, plan_pro, is_active=False)

        for i in range(2):
            _make_workspace(f'ws-empty-{i}')

        with django_assert_num_queries(30, exact=False):
            response = admin_client.get(LIST_URL)

        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.data['count'] == 10
        assert len(_results(response)) == 10

from django.urls import path, include
from rest_framework.routers import DefaultRouter

from apps.console.api.views.membership_views import MembershipViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.governance_views import GovernanceViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.capability_views import CapabilityViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.plan_views import PlanViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.feature_views import FeatureViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.plan_feature_views import PlanFeatureViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.provider_views import (
    ProviderViewSet,
    ProviderConnectionViewSet,
    ProviderContractViewSet,
)
from apps.console.api.views.provider_pricing_views import ProviderPricingViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.usage_views import UsageMetricViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.usage_record_views import UsageRecordViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.provider_log_views import ProviderLogViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.cost_views import CostViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.workspace_admin_views import AdminWorkspaceViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.user_admin_views import AdminUserViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.storage_views import StorageViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.quota_views import QuotaViewSet
from apps.console.api.views.storage_budget_views import StorageBudgetViewSet

router = DefaultRouter()

# Console V1 — acesso e governança
router.register(r'members', MembershipViewSet, basename='member')
router.register(r'governances', GovernanceViewSet, basename='governance')
router.register(r'capabilities', CapabilityViewSet, basename='capability')

# Console V1 — comercial
router.register(r'plans', PlanViewSet, basename='plan')
router.register(r'features', FeatureViewSet, basename='feature')
router.register(r'plan-features', PlanFeatureViewSet, basename='plan-feature')

# Console V1 — providers
router.register(r'providers', ProviderViewSet, basename='provider')
router.register(r'connections', ProviderConnectionViewSet, basename='connection')
router.register(r'contracts', ProviderContractViewSet, basename='contract')
router.register(r'pricings', ProviderPricingViewSet, basename='pricing')

# Console V1 — uso e logs
router.register(r'metrics', UsageMetricViewSet, basename='metric')
router.register(r'usage-records', UsageRecordViewSet, basename='usage-record')
router.register(r'provider-logs', ProviderLogViewSet, basename='provider-log')

# Console V1 — economia
router.register(r'costs', CostViewSet, basename='cost')

# Console V1 — orçamento operacional de armazenamento
# Registrado ANTES de 'storage' para que a rota mais específica
# (`storage/budget/`) não seja capturada pelo `StorageViewSet`.
router.register(
    r'storage/budget',
    StorageBudgetViewSet,
    basename='storage-budget',
)

# Console V1 — armazenamento (snapshot read-only)
router.register(r'storage', StorageViewSet, basename='storage')

# Console V1 — quota de armazenamento
router.register(r'quota', QuotaViewSet, basename='quota')

# Console V1 — visão administrativa (read-only)
router.register(r'workspaces', AdminWorkspaceViewSet, basename='admin-workspace')
router.register(r'users', AdminUserViewSet, basename='admin-user')

urlpatterns = [
    path('api/console/', include(router.urls)),
]

"""
View do Console.

Autorização:
    ConsoleMembership concede acesso administrativo integral.
    Não usar is_superuser como regra de negócio.
"""

from django.shortcuts import render, redirect
from django.views import View
import logging

from apps.console.services.membership_service import ConsoleMembershipService

logger = logging.getLogger('console')


class ConsoleView(View):
    """View para a página do Console."""

    def get(self, request):
        if not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return redirect('/login/')

        service = ConsoleMembershipService()
        if not service.has_access(request.user):
            logger.debug(
                f"⛔ Usuário {request.user.email} sem ConsoleMembership "
                f"tentou acessar /console/ — redirecionando para /mesa/"
            )
            return redirect('/mesa/')

        return render(request, 'console.html')
